using System.Collections.Generic;
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules;

public class SlimeBodyItemDropRule : IItemDropRule
{
	public List<IItemDropRuleChainAttempt> ChainedRules { get; private set; }

	public SlimeBodyItemDropRule()
	{
		ChainedRules = new List<IItemDropRuleChainAttempt>();
	}

	public bool CanDrop(DropAttemptInfo info)
	{
		if (NPCID.Sets.SlimeCanContainItems[info.npc.type] && info.npc.ai[1] > 0f)
		{
			return info.npc.ai[1] < (float)ItemID.Count;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public ItemDropAttemptResult TryDroppingItem(DropAttemptInfo info)
	{
		int itemId = (int)info.npc.ai[1];
		GetDropInfo(itemId, out var amountDroppedMinimum, out var amountDroppedMaximum);
		CommonCode.DropItemFromNPC(info.npc, itemId, info.rng.Next(amountDroppedMinimum, amountDroppedMaximum + 1));
		return new ItemDropAttemptResult
		{
			State = ItemDropAttemptResultState.Success
		};
	}

	public void GetDropInfo(int itemId, out int amountDroppedMinimum, out int amountDroppedMaximum)
	{
		amountDroppedMinimum = 1;
		amountDroppedMaximum = 1;
		switch (itemId)
		{
		case 8:
			amountDroppedMinimum = 5;
			amountDroppedMaximum = 10;
			break;
		case 166:
			amountDroppedMinimum = 2;
			amountDroppedMaximum = 6;
			break;
		case 965:
			amountDroppedMinimum = 20;
			amountDroppedMaximum = 45;
			break;
		case 11:
		case 12:
		case 13:
		case 14:
		case 174:
		case 364:
		case 365:
		case 366:
		case 699:
		case 700:
		case 701:
		case 702:
		case 1104:
		case 1105:
		case 1106:
		case 3347:
			amountDroppedMinimum = 3;
			amountDroppedMaximum = 13;
			break;
		case 71:
			amountDroppedMinimum = 50;
			amountDroppedMaximum = 99;
			break;

using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Terraria.ID;
using Terraria.Utilities;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules;

public static class CommonCode
{
	public static void DropItemFromNPC(NPC npc, int itemId, int stack, bool scattered = false)
	{
		if (itemId > 0 && itemId < ItemID.Count)
		{
			int x = (int)npc.position.X + npc.width / 2;
			int y = (int)npc.position.Y + npc.height / 2;
			if (scattered)
			{
				x = (int)npc.position.X + Main.rand.Next(npc.width + 1);
				y = (int)npc.position.Y + Main.rand.Next(npc.height + 1);
			}
			int itemIndex = Item.NewItem(npc.GetItemSource_Loot(), x, y, 0, 0, itemId, stack, noBroadcast: false, -1);
			ModifyItemDropFromNPC(npc, itemIndex);
		}
	}

	public static void DropItemLocalPerClientAndSetNPCMoneyTo0(NPC npc, int itemId, int stack, bool interactionRequired = true)
	{
		if (itemId <= 0 || itemId >= ItemID.Count)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (Main.netMode == 2)
		{
			int num = Item.NewItem(npc.GetItemSource_Loot(), (int)npc.position.X, (int)npc.position.Y, npc.width, npc.height, itemId, stack, noBroadcast: true, -1);
			Main.timeItemSlotCannotBeReusedFor[num] = 54000;
			for (int i = 0; i < 255; i++)
			{
				if (Main.player[i].active && (npc.playerInteraction[i] || !interactionRequired))
				{
					NetMessage.SendData(90, i, -1, null, num);
				}
			}
			Main.item[num].TurnToAir();
		}
		else
		{
			DropItemFromNPC(npc, itemId, stack);
		}
		npc.value = 0f;
	}

	public static void DropItemForEachInteractingPlayerOnThePlayer(NPC npc, int itemId, UnifiedRandom rng, int chanceNumerator, int chanceDenominator, int stack = 1, bool interactionRequired = true)
	{
		if (itemId <= 0 || itemId >= ItemID.Count)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (Main.netMode == 2)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < 255; i++)
			{
				Player player = Main.player[i];
				if (player.active && (npc.playerInteraction[i] || !interactionRequired) && rng.Next(chanceDenominator) < chanceNumerator)
				{
					int itemIndex = Item.NewItem(npc.GetItemSource_Loot(), player.position, player.Size, itemId, stack, noBroadcast: false, -1);
					ModifyItemDropFromNPC(npc, itemIndex);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (rng.Next(chanceDenominator) < chanceNumerator)
		{
			DropItemFromNPC(npc, itemId, stack);
		}
		npc.value = 0f;
	}

	public static void ModifyItemDropFromNPC(NPC npc, int itemIndex)
	{
		WorldItem worldItem = Main.item[itemIndex];
		switch (worldItem.type)
		{
		case 23:
			if (npc.type == 1 && npc.netID != -1 && npc.netID != -2 && npc.netID != -5 && npc.netID != -6)
			{
				worldItem.color = npc.color;
				NetMessage.SendData(88, -1, -1, null, itemIndex, 1f);
			}
			if (Main.remixWorld && npc.type == 59)
			{
				worldItem.color = new Color(255, 127, 0);
				NetMessage.SendData(88, -1, -1, null, itemIndex, 1f);

```

  private slimeColorTick(game: GameHooks) {
    if (this.vanillaId !== 1 || !this.colorRGBA) return;
    // ---- 踩油漆扫描（NPC.cs:60973-60983）----
    // j 自 floor(Left.X/16) 至 floor(Right.X/16)（含端点，跨格史莱姆每格各 lerp一次）；
    // num10 = floor((Bottom.Y+1)/16)（底边下一格）；门槛 = SolidTile3（:70470-70489
    // active && !inActive && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop）&& color>0 && color!=30（负相不吃）
    const st = game.world.store;
    let flag = false;
    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h + 1) / TILE);
    for (let j = Math.floor(this.x / TILE); j <= Math.floor((this.x + this.w) / TILE); j++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(j, ty)) continue;
      const i = st.idx(j, ty);
      const t = st.type[i];
      // SolidTile3：非活性格/已致动/非实心/平台（tileSolidTop）都不算立足点
      if (!(st.flags[i] && t !== 0)) continue;
      if (st.wire[i] & 32) continue;                       // inActive（致动缩入）
      const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
      if (!d?.solid || d.platform) continue;
      const pc = st.paint[i];
      if (pc > 0 && pc !== 30) {
        const c = paintColor(pc);                          // WorldGen.paintColor :60979
        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) this.colorRGBA[k] = (this.colorRGBA[k] * 19 + c[k]) / 20;
        this.colorRGBA[3] = (this.colorRGBA[3] * 19 + 100) / 20; // :60980 alpha 目标恒 100
        flag = true;
      }
    }
    // ---- 体内物品色（NPC.cs:60984-61086：ai[1]>0 且本轮无踩漆才生效） ----
    if (this.ai1 <= 0 || flag) return;
    const t = Enemy.SLIME_ITEM_COLORS[this.ai1];
    if (!t) return;
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) this.colorRGBA[k] = (this.colorRGBA[k] * 19 + t[k]) / 20;
    this.colorRGBA[3] = (this.colorRGBA[3] * 19 + t[3]) / 20;
  }

   *  加速度 0.1、最大速度 1.0（原版 num1/num2 常量），门/高门交互待移植 */

  doorWarm = 0;            // 攻门蓄势（AI_003 ai[0]：站定累计 60）
  doorHit = 0;             // 攻门累计（AI_003 ai[1]：每 tick +5 至 10 尝试）
  doorCd = 0;              // 脱离冷却（AI_003 ai[3]=num21=30t）

  dummyHitDir = 0;         // localAI[1]：受击方向
  dummyAnchor: [number, number] | null = null;  // ai[0]/ai[1]：tile 378 锚点
  private dummyAI(game: GameHooks) {
    const st = game.world.store;
    const [ax, ay] = this.dummyAnchor ?? [-1, -1];
    // 锚 tile 378 消失 → 失活（cs:40886-40889）
    if (ax < 0 || !st.inBounds(ax, ay) || st.type[st.idx(ax, ay)] !== DUMMY_TILE_ID) {
      this.dead = true;
      return;
    }
    // 摇晃衰减（FindFrame 488 分支 cs:71516-71544 同步逻辑在渲染帧端）
    if (this.dummyShake > 0) this.dummyShake--;
    // 静止：仅重力站立
    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
    this.vx = 0;
    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);

  }

  /** UpdateNPC_CastLights（NPC.cs:93520-93603）敌怪侧两段：发光蘑菇族闪烁蓝光 +
   *  44 不死矿工头盔灯。城镇 NPC 段（160 松露 / 209 电子人）归 TownNPC 实体不在此。
   *  蘑菇族（:93576-93588）：num9 = Next(28,42)×0.005 + (270−mouseTextColor)/500，
   *  基色 (0.1, 0.3+num9/2, 0.6+num9) × num13（634 孢子蝙蝠 0.65，其余 0.35）。 */
  private updateNpcCastLights() {
    const vid = this.vanillaId;
    if (vid === null) return;
    if ((vid >= 254 && vid <= 261) || vid === 634 || vid === 635) {
      const num9 = (28 + Math.random() * 14) * 0.005 + (270 - mouseTextColorApprox()) / 500;
      const num13 = vid === 634 ? 0.65 : 0.35;
      this.mergeLightRGB([0.1 * num13, (0.3 + num9 / 2) * num13, (0.6 + num9) * num13]);
      // 256 菌鱼与 AI_018 水母光（cs:24276）原版同帧叠加——单槽逐通道 max
    } else if (vid === 44) {
      // :93599-93602 不死矿工：(0.9, 0.75, 0.5) @ (position.x+w/2, position.y+4)
      this.lightRGB = [0.9, 0.75, 0.5];
      this.lightRGBAt = { x: this.cx, y: this.y + 4 };
    }
  }

  /** UpdateNPC_UpdateTrails TrailingMode==4（NPC.cs:91825-91841）：120 混沌元素 /
   *  137 荧光蝠 / 138 荧光史莱姆——循环体内在 position（顶左格）加 (0.3,0,0.2)。 */
  private updateNpcTrailLights() {
    const vid = this.vanillaId;
    if (vid !== 120 && vid !== 137 && vid !== 138) return;
    this.lightRGB = [0.3, 0, 0.2];
    this.lightRGBAt = { x: this.x, y: this.y };
  }

  /** debuff 自发光（NPC.cs:92181-92467，DrawNPC 前的 buff 视效段）：onFire/onFire3/
   *  daybreak/onFire2(诅咒焰) (1,0.3,0.1)、betsysCurse (0.6,0.1,0.9)、onFrostBurn/
   *  onFrostBurn2 (0.1,0.6,1)。位置 = (position.x, position.y+16)。原版同帧可与
   *  type 光叠加；本仓单槽 → 逐通道 max 合并（AI 已写 lightRGBAt 时不挪光源位） */
  /** 单槽点光的通道 max 合并：AI 已写 lightRGB 时逐通道取大（原版 AddLight 可同帧
   *  叠加，单槽下 max 最接近视觉并集）且不挪既有 lightRGBAt；空槽则直接写入 */
  private mergeLightRGB(rgb: [number, number, number], at?: { x: number; y: number }) {
    if (this.lightRGB) {
      this.lightRGB = [
        Math.max(this.lightRGB[0], rgb[0]),
        Math.max(this.lightRGB[1], rgb[1]),
        Math.max(this.lightRGB[2], rgb[2]),
      ];
    } else {
      this.lightRGB = rgb;
      if (at) this.lightRGBAt = at;
    }
  }

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T09:45:27.982Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
// 彩蛋种子解析（对齐原版 WorldGen.CheckSpecialWorldSeed / UIWorldCreation.ProcessSeed）。
// 匹配规则：去分隔符后小写比较（原版语义）。

export interface SeedFlags {
  crimsonForce?: boolean;    // not the bees：强制猩红
  getGoodWorld?: boolean;    // for the worthy
  drunkWorld?: boolean;      // 05162020 / drunk world
  noTraps?: boolean;         // no traps
  theConstant?: boolean;     // the constant（dontstarve）
  tenthAnniversary?: boolean;// celebrationmk10
  remix?: boolean;           // don't dig up
  everything?: boolean;      // getfixedboi（组合种子）
  skyblock?: boolean;        // 1.4.5.6 新增 skyblock
  notTheBees?: boolean;
  infectedSeed?: boolean;    // 1.4.5 worldIsInfected 秘密种子（Main.infectedSeed）——
  //  尚无文本种子映射到该位（注册为哈希种子，WorldGen.cs:374），仅作 SceneMetrics
  //  向日葵压制 ×3 等读取位的预留（恒 false 时行为与原版普通世界一致）
  graveyardBloodmoonStart?: boolean; // SecretSeed.graveyardBloodmoonStart（WorldGen.cs:356，
  //  哈希注册无文本种子）——墓园 pass 的第二触发源（:18117-18120），预留位恒 false
  //  时行为与原版普通世界一致
}

const TABLE: Array<[RegExp, (f: SeedFlags) => void]> = [
  [/^notthebees$/i, (f) => { f.notTheBees = true; f.crimsonForce = true; }],
  [/^fortheworthy$/i, (f) => { f.getGoodWorld = true; }],
  [/^(05162020|drunkworld)$/i, (f) => { f.drunkWorld = true; }],
  [/^notraps$/i, (f) => { f.noTraps = true; }],
  [/^(theconstant|constant|eye4aneye|eyeforaneye)$/i, (f) => { f.theConstant = true; }],
  [/^(celebrationmk10|5162021|5162011)$/i, (f) => { f.tenthAnniversary = true; }],
  [/^dontdigup$/i, (f) => { f.remix = true; }],
  [/^getfixedboi$/i, (f) => { f.everything = true; f.drunkWorld = true; f.getGoodWorld = true; f.notTheBees = true; }],
  [/^skyblock$/i, (f) => { f.skyblock = true; }],  // 1.4.5.6 WorldGenerationOptions 新增
];

export function parseSeed(rawSeed: string): { flags: SeedFlags; seedText: string; /** UI 展示用特殊世界名（无则 null） */ specialName: string | null } {
  const normalized = rawSeed.replace(/[\s._-]/g, '').toLowerCase();
  const flags: SeedFlags = {};
  for (const [re, apply] of TABLE) {
    if (re.test(normalized)) {
      apply(flags);
      break;
    }
  }
  // 原版还支持「大小.难度.邪恶.种子」复合格式（如 1.2.1.abc）——保留原文作 seed
  const specialName = flags.everything ? 'get fixed boi'
    : flags.drunkWorld ? '05162020'
    : flags.notTheBees ? 'not the bees'
    : flags.getGoodWorld ? 'for the worthy'
    : flags.theConstant ? 'the constant'
    : flags.remix ? "don't dig up"
    : flags.skyblock ? 'skyblock'
    : flags.tenthAnniversary ? 'celebrationmk10'
    : flags.noTraps ? 'no traps'
    : null;
  return { flags, seedText: rawSeed, specialName };
}

```

          value: this.spawnedFromStatue ? 0

        // 已击败 + 目标玩家身处地牢，击杀 lifeMax>100、value>0、非 288 的敌怪 →

}

  /** 岩浆专属冷却(原版 immune[255]=30,Collision_LavaCollision :94522) */

  constructor(key: string, x: number, y: number) {
    super();
    this.npcKey = key;
    this.x = x;
    this.y = y - this.h;
    const town = TOWN_NPC_IDS[key];
    this.vanillaId = town?.id ?? 22; // 未登记 key 兜底向导贴图
    this.givenName = newNpcName(this.vanillaId);
    const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(this.vanillaId)];
    this.maxHp = this.hp = v?.lifeMax ?? 250;
  }

  /** 被陷阱弹幕等命中：dmg - defense*0.5（下限 1），8 tick 无敌帧；
   *  死亡即移除——"重生"由入驻轮承担（原版 UpdateTime_SpawnTownNPCs：不在场且
   *  条件满足 → 重新入驻，≈次日回归），无需独立重生系统 */
  hurt(dmg: number, kbx: number, _kby: number, game: GameHooks): boolean {
    if (this.iframes > 0 || this.dead) return false;
    const books = (game.world.flags.combatBookWasUsed ? 1 : 0) + (game.world.flags.combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed ? 1 : 0);
    const def = (VANILLA_NPCS[String(this.vanillaId)]?.defense ?? 15) + books * 8; // NPC.cs:53419
    dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - def * 0.5));
    this.hp -= dmg;
    this.iframes = 8;
    game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.y, dmg, false, '#FFD060');
    // 受击血粒子(原版 StrikeNPC 尘近似,与 Enemy.hurt 同规)
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#B01818', 5, 1.4, { life: 22, grav: 0.06 });
    this.vx += kbx * 0.6;
    if (this.hp <= 0) {
      this.dead = true;
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#C03030', 8, 2);
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#8A1818', 4, 1.2);
      game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_1'], 0.8);
    } else {
      game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Hit_1'], 0.6);
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** 战斗书增益（NPC.cs:53419-53430：每本 lifeMax+250 / 伤害出力×0.8 / 移速+0.25 /
   *  防+8——伤害/移速两项无对应战斗系统，落 lifeMax/防；每本可叠加，由调用方按旗标次数调） */
  applyCombatBook(): void {
    this.maxHp += 250;
    this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + 250);
  }

  private cbApplied = false;

  /** 本 tick 点光（NPC.cs 城镇 NPC 侧三处恒亮）：
   *  - 160 松露（UpdateNPC_CastLights :93544-93566）：num3=Next(28,42)×0.005+
   *    (270−mouseTextColor)/500，基色 (0.1,0.3+num3/2,0.6+num3)×0.35；变体 1
   *    （townNpcVariationIndex==1）R/B 互换——本仓不跟踪变体档，取默认档
   *  - 209 电子人（:93590-93599）：同式抖动 ×0.35、B 基 0.4（ai[0]==12 的
   *    Top 追加光 (0.3,0.1,0.1) 未接——音乐盒状态未移植，登记 GAP）
   *  - 681 彩虹城镇史莱姆（AI_007 尾段 :55963-55974）= TorchColor(23)×0.35
   *  - 683 黄城镇史莱姆（:55974-55979）= (0.25,0.25,0.1)×WrappedLerp(0.75,1,t%120/120)
   *  条件态三处（:53637 微光上升 / :55395·:55547 魔法光环）依赖未移植状态机，跳过 */
  get lightRGB(): [number, number, number] | null {
    if (this.vanillaId === 160) {
      const n3 = (28 + Math.random() * 14) * 0.005 + (270 - mouseTextColorApprox()) / 500;
      return [0.1 * 0.35, (0.3 + n3 / 2) * 0.35, (0.6 + n3) * 0.35];
    }
    if (this.vanillaId === 209) {
      const n3 = (28 + Math.random() * 14) * 0.005 + (270 - mouseTextColorApprox()) / 500;
      return [0.1 * 0.35, (0.3 + n3 / 2) * 0.35, (0.4 + n3 / 2) * 0.35];
    }
    if (this.vanillaId === 681) {
      const num = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.9 + (270 - mouseTextColorApprox()) / 900));
      return [num * 0.9 * 0.35, 0.55 * 0.35, num * 1.2 * 0.35];   // TorchColor(23)×0.35
    }
    if (this.vanillaId === 683) {
      const p = (visEffectFrames() % 120) / 120;
      const tri = p * 2 > 1 ? 2 - p * 2 : p * 2;                   // Utils.WrappedLerp
      const l = 0.75 + 0.25 * tri;
      return [0.25 * l, 0.25 * l, 0.1 * l];
    }
    return null;
  }
  lightRGBAt: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;

  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    this.animT++;
    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
    const world = game.world;
    // 战斗书增益一次性套用（后入驻的 NPC 在首 tick 补；使用书时对在场者即时叠）
    if (!this.cbApplied) {
      this.cbApplied = true;
      const books0 = (world.flags.combatBookWasUsed ? 1 : 0) + (world.flags.combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed ? 1 : 0);
      for (let k = 0; k < books0; k++) this.applyCombatBook();
    }

    // 微光化城镇变体（NPC.GetShimmered :92560-92573：ShimmerTownTransform → ai[0]=25

   *  - 入态:ai[1]=AttackTime,面向目标;态内每 tick ai[1]--/localAI[3]++/vx×0.8
   *  - 发射档:localAI[3]==num46/55/65;连发链 `localAI[3]>档 → 推下一档`(:55066 族)
   *  - 退出:ai[1]<=0 → 冷却 localAI[1] = cdBase/2 + rand(cdRand)(:54927-54934 族)
   *  近似项:难度伤害缩放恒 Classic=1(Journey/Expert 切换未接);633 狼人变体/
   *  108 连发 1-3/160·663 落点弹重掷等特化在发射端按 note 近似 */
  private attackUpdate(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks): boolean {
    if (this.atkCd > 0) this.atkCd--;
    const atkType = TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[this.vanillaId];
    if (atkType === undefined) return false;
    this.hardModeFlag = !!(world.flags as Record<string, boolean>).hardMode;
    if (this.atkState === 0) {
      // 触发门:站地 + 冷却尽 + 探测范围内有 LOS 敌(:55621 flag31 + :55864 族掷骰)
      if (!this.onGround || this.atkCd > 0 || this.talking) return false;
      const range = TOWN_DANGER_RANGE[this.vanillaId] ?? 200;
      const target = this.pickSideTarget(world, game, range);
      if (!target) return false;
      let chance = TOWN_ATTACK_CHANCE[this.vanillaId] ?? 1;
      let num3 = 2;                             // :53413(Skyblock ÷1.5 未接)
      if (this.vanillaId === 209) num3 /= 3;    // :53548 蒸汽朋克人三倍勤
      const gate = Math.max(1, Math.floor(chance * num3));
      if (Math.random() >= 1 / gate) return false;  // rand(gate)==0
      const params = this.atkParams(atkType);
      if (!params && atkType !== 3) return false;
      this.atkState = atkType === 0 ? 10 : atkType === 1 ? 12 : atkType === 2 ? 14 : 15;
      this.atkTimer = TOWN_ATTACK_TIME[this.vanillaId] ?? 30;
      this.atkTick = 0;
      this.atkBurstIdx = 0;
      this.atkNextShot = (params as TownShotParams | undefined)?.shootTick ?? 1;
      this.facing = (target as { cx: number }).cx < this.cx ? -1 : 1;
      return true;
    }
    // ---- 态内推进 ----
    this.atkTimer--;
    this.atkTick++;
    this.vx *= 0.8;
    const range = TOWN_DANGER_RANGE[this.vanillaId] ?? 200;
    if (this.atkState === 15) {
      this.swingUpdate(world, game);
    } else {
      const params = this.atkParams(TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[this.vanillaId]) as TownShotParams;
      // 连发链:localAI[3] 超过当前档 → 推下一档(原版逐档 if 链)
      const burst = params.burst ?? [];
      while (this.atkBurstIdx < burst.length && this.atkTick > this.atkNextShot) {
        this.atkNextShot = burst[this.atkBurstIdx++];
      }
      if (this.atkTick === this.atkNextShot) this.shootAt(world, game, params, range);
    }
    if (this.atkTimer <= 0) {
      // 退出 + 冷却(:54927/:55199/:55397/:55529 族):localAI[1] = cdBase/2 + rand(cdRand)
      const p = this.atkParams(TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[this.vanillaId]);
      const cdBase = (p as TownShotParams | undefined)?.cdBase ?? 10;
      const cdRand = (p as TownShotParams | undefined)?.cdRand ?? 1;
      this.atkState = 0;
      this.atkCd = Math.floor(cdBase / 2) + Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(1, cdRand));
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** 当前类型的态参数(10/12/14;15 走 TOWN_SWING);hardMode 覆盖按 world.flags */
  private atkParams(atkType: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3): TownShotParams | undefined {
    const table = atkType === 0 ? TOWN_MELEE : atkType === 1 ? TOWN_BOW : atkType === 2 ? TOWN_MAGIC : undefined;
    if (!table) return undefined;
    const base = table[this.vanillaId];
    if (!base) return undefined;
    if (base.hardMode && this.hardModeFlag) return { ...base, ...base.hardMode } as TownShotParams;
    return base;
  }

import dropData from '../data/vanilla-npcdrops.json';
import npcValueJson from '../data/vanilla-npcvalue.json';
import type { RNG } from '../core/rng';

/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any */
type Rule = any;
const ENTRIES: Array<{ npcs?: number[]; global?: boolean; rule: Rule }> = (dropData as any).entries;
const SLIME_BODY: Record<string, [number, number]> = (dropData as any).slimeBody;
const NPC_VALUE: Record<string, number> = npcValueJson as any;

/** netID → 规则列表（注册序）；原版 _entriesByNpcNetId */
const rulesByNpc = new Map<number, Rule[]>();
const globalRules: Rule[] = [];
for (const e of ENTRIES) {
  if (e.global) { globalRules.push(e.rule); continue; }
  for (const n of e.npcs ?? []) {
    let l = rulesByNpc.get(n);
    if (!l) rulesByNpc.set(n, l = []);
    l.push(e.rule);
  }
}

export function npcValueOf(netIdOrType: number): number {
  return NPC_VALUE[String(netIdOrType)] ?? 0;
}

  private tryTransformTo(id: number, st: GameHooks['world']['store'], allowEmbedded = false): boolean {
    const v = vanillaNpc(id);

    if (!allowEmbedded) {
      for (let tx = Math.floor(nx / TILE); tx <= Math.floor((nx + nw - 0.01) / TILE); tx++) {

          if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return false;
        }
      }

    this.vanillaId = id;
    this.vanilla = v;
    this.w = nw; this.h = nh;
    this.x = nx; this.y = ny;
    this.maxHp = v.lifeMax;

    this.def = {
      ...this.def,

  private slimeGenItemInsideBody(isBallooned: boolean): number {
    const roll = (n: number) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);       // Next(n)

        case 5: return 4612; case 6: return 4674;
        case 7: case 8: case 9: return 4343;

    }
  }

 "old_string": "  /** AI_001_Slimes 原版核（1.4.5.6 NPC.cs:60735+）：\n   *  站定 vx*=0.8；ai[0] 每 tick +1（激愤 +1 额外）；\n   *  阶段判定（num54=-1000）：ai0>=0 → 小跳（vy=-6, vx+=2*dir, ai0=-1120）；\n   *  -1000..-500 → 小跳（ai0=-2120）；-2000..-1500 → 大跳（vy=-8, vx+=3*dir, ai0=-200）。\n   *  即：小跳→小跳→大跳循环，约 5-10 秒节奏。\n   *  flag3 激愤（L61446）= 夜晚 || 受过伤 || 地下 → 追玩家；白天满血地表 = 被动保持方向。\n   *  卡墙（L61937）：落地 X == 起跳 X → direction 反转 + ai[2]=200 冷却（期间不索敌）。\n   *  蚱蜢(377/446) 复用本 AI（ai0 额外 +3，跳得更频繁） */\n  private slimeAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {",
 "new_string": "  /** NPCID.Sets.SlimeCanContainItems（NPCID.cs:4791）：可携带体内物品的史莱姆族 */\n  private static readonly SLIME_CAN_CONTAIN_ITEMS = new Set([1, 59, 147, 184, 537]);\n\n  /** AI_001_Slimes_GenerateItemInsideBody（NPC.cs:62150-62258）1:1：num=Next(4) 四桶；\n   *  isBallooned（风气球 ai[0]==-999，:62154）→ 13 桶风筝/纸飞机。\n   *  WorldGen.Skyblock.lowTiles 三处修正（:62156-62164 / :62199-62207 / :62230-62237）\n   *  依赖天空盒世界——本仓无天空盒世界（NpcDrops evalCond 'SkyblockIsUp' 同判恒\n   *  false）→ 跳过。case 0 default 的 netMode!=0 分支（多人 2997 虫洞药水）单人不可达 */\n  private slimeGenItemInsideBody(isBallooned: boolean): number {\n    const roll = (n: number) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);              // Next(n)\n    const rollR = (a: number, b: number) => a + Math.floor(Math.random() * (b - a)); // Next(a,b) 含a不含b\n    let num = roll(4);\n    if (isBallooned) {\n      switch (roll(13)) {                                                     // :62166-62191 风筝桶\n        case 1: return 4368; case 2: return 4369; case 3: return 4370; case 4: return 4371;\n        case 5: return 4612; case 6: return 4674;\n        case 7: case 8: case 9: return 4343;\n        case 10: case 11: case 12: return 4344;\n        default: return 4367;\n      }\n    }\n    switch (num) {\n      case 0:                                                                // 药水桶 :62193\n        switch (roll(7)) {\n          case 0: return 290;            // 迅捷\n          case 1: return 292;            // 铁皮\n          case 2: return 296;            // 光标\n          case 3: return 2322;           // 挖矿\n          default: return 2350;          // 回忆（2997 虫洞仅多人，:62200-62203）\n        }\n      case 1:                                                                // 火把/炸弹桶 :62196\n        num = roll(4);\n        return num === 0 ? 8 : num === 1 ? 965 : num === 2 ? 166 : 58;        // 火把/荧光棒/炸弹/红心\n      case 2: {                                                              // 矿物桶 :62222\n        if (roll(2) === 0) return rollR(11, 15);   // 11-14 铜/铁/银/金矿\n        return rollR(699, 703);                    // 699-702 同序锭\n      }\n      default:                                                                // 钱币桶 :62239\n        num = roll(3);\n        return num === 0 ? 71 : num === 1 ? 72 : 73;   // 铜/银/金币（堆叠档在掉落侧）\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** AI_001_Slimes 体内物品主掷段（NPC.cs:60750-60960）：首 AI 帧 ai[1]==0 且 value>0\n   *  时掷内容物——先置 -1（空）再最多 num2 次机会，任一分支赋正值后即停（:60777-60781）。\n   *  netID -5/-4（地牢/丛林彩装变种）整体不掷（:60815 continue），-6 额外 +4 次\n   *  （:60769），-10（蜜蜂丛林变种）走 1124/1125/314/5395 特例（:60840），史莱姆雨\n   *  +2 次（:60773）。跳过分支（登记）：\n   *  - WorldGen.Skyblock.{lowTiles,noHellstone,noFossils,noLifeCrystals} 六处（:60757/\n   *    :60806/:60813/:60822/:60827/:60859/:60892）——天空盒世界未实装恒 false\n   *  - Item.GetRandomVoiceItem（:60828-60830，天空盒子分支内）——连带跳过\n   *  - Main.vampireSeed（:60920-60923 地下 1/13 → 9 木头）——1.4.5 vampirism 哈希\n   *    秘密种子无文本映射（SeedFlags 无位，参照 infectedSeed 预留位惯例） */\n  private slimeRollContainedItem(game: GameHooks): void {\n    const vid = this.vanillaId;\n    if (vid === null || !Enemy.SLIME_CAN_CONTAIN_ITEMS.has(vid)) return;\n    if (this.ai1 !== 0) return;                    // 已掷过：-1=空 / >0=物品（只掷一次）\n    const netId = this.vanillaNetId !== 0 ? this.vanillaNetId : vid;\n    const value = this.spawnedFromStatue ? 0                       // 与死亡掉落链同式（:6258 侧）\n      : (netId === vid ? (this.def.value ?? npcValueOf(netId)) : npcValueOf(netId));\n    if (value <= 0) return;                        // :60750 value>0 门\n    const w = game.world;\n    const sf = w.seedFlags;\n    this.ai1 = -1;                                 // :60752 先置空\n    const roll = (n: number) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);\n    let maxValue = 20;                             // :60753 主桶分母\n    let maxValue2 = 40;                            // :60754 副桶分母\n    let num2 = 1;                                  // :60755 掷骰次数\n    // WorldGen.Skyblock.lowTiles（:60757-60768：maxValue2=20/num2+4/maxValue=15，-6 再+5）\n    // ——天空盒世界未实装，跳过\n    if (netId === -6) num2 += 4;                   // :60769-60772\n    if (w.weather.slimeRain) num2 += 2;            // :60773-60775 Main.slimeRain\n    const surfaceY = w.groundLevel;                // Main.worldSurface（tile）\n    const yTile = this.y / TILE;\n    const rockLayer = (y: number): boolean => (sf.remix   // WorldGen.isThisInTheRockLayer :26916\n      ? (y > surfaceY && y <= w.rockLevel) : y > w.rockLevel);\n    for (let i = 0; i < num2; i++) {\n      if (this.ai1 !== -1) break;                  // :60777-60781 已中即停\n      if (vid === 59) {                            // :60784 岩浆史莱姆\n        if (sf.remix && roll(maxValue) === 0) {    // Main.remixWorld\n          this.ai1 = this.slimeGenItemInsideBody(this.ai0 === -999);\n        }\n        // else if Skyblock.noHellstone && downedBoss3 && Next(15)==0 → 174 地狱石（:60789-60793）\n        //   ——天空盒未实装跳过\n      } else if (yTile > surfaceY && (vid === 147 || vid === 184)) {  // :60796 地下棘刺史莱姆\n        // 两 lowTiles 子分支（:60800-60811）与普通分支同体 → 合并\n        if (roll(maxValue2) === 0) this.ai1 = roll(2) === 0 ? 1103 : 593;  // 淤泥/雪块\n      } else if (vid === 537) {                    // :60812 沙漠史莱姆\n        // Skyblock.noFossils && Next(5)==0 → 3347 沙漠化石（:60813-60816）：天空盒未实装跳过\n      } else {\n        if (vid !== 1 || netId === -5 || netId === -4) continue;   // :60815 仅蓝史莱姆族\n        let num4 = 500;                            // :60817 史莱姆法杖分母\n        if (sf.noTraps) num4 = 20;                 // Main.noTrapsWorld\n        else if (sf.getGoodWorld) num4 = 100;      // Main.getGoodWorld\n        else if (this.cy < surfaceY * TILE) num4 = -1;             // 地表蓝史莱姆不掷法杖\n        // isThisInTheRockLayer && (noLifeCrystals||lowTiles) && !AnyLifeCrystalSlimes() &&\n        //   Next(200)==0 → 29 生命水晶（:60822-60826）：天空盒未实装跳过\n        // Skyblock.lowTiles && 地下 && Next(1000)==0 → 语音物品（:60827-60830）：同上连带跳过\n        const genuineParty = !!(game as unknown as { partyState?: { genuineParty?: boolean } })\n          .partyState?.genuineParty;               // BirthdayParty.GenuineParty（真派对）\n        if (genuineParty && this.cy < surfaceY * TILE) {            // :60831-60839 地表真派对\n          this.ai1 = roll(2) === 0 ? 3736 + roll(3) : 1345;         // Next(3736,3739) 气球/彩纸\n        } else if (netId === -10 && roll(20) === 0) {               // :60840-60849 蜜蜂丛林变种\n          const num5 = sf.notTheBees ? roll(4) : roll(3);           // notTheBeesWorld 扩到 314/5395\n          this.ai1 = num5 === 1 ? 1125 : num5 === 2 ? 314 : num5 === 3 ? 5395 : 1124;\n        } else if (roll(maxValue) === 0) {                          // :60850-60854 主桶\n          this.ai1 = this.slimeGenItemInsideBody(this.ai0 === -999);\n        } else if (roll(maxValue2) === 0) {                         // :60855 副桶\n          if (yTile <= surfaceY) {\n            // Skyblock.lowTiles 表层分支（:60859-60876：194/195/62/27）：天空盒未实装跳过\n            this.ai1 = 751;                        // 云块\n          } else if (!rockLayer(yTile)) {                            // :60882-60887 浅地下\n            const num7 = roll(3);\n            this.ai1 = num7 === 1 ? 3 : num7 === 2 ? 9 : 2;         // 石/木/土\n          } else if (roll(10) === 0) {\n            this.ai1 = 3609;                        // :60888-60890 传送带\n          } else {\n            // Skyblock.lowTiles && hardMode 矿桶（:60892-60902）：天空盒未实装跳过\n            const num9 = roll(4);                   // :60904-60910 深层\n            this.ai1 = num9 === 1 ? 150 : num9 === 2 ? 3086 : num9 === 3 ? 3081 : 3; // 蛛网/花岗岩/大理石/石\n          }\n        } else if (num4 > 0 && roll(num4) === 0) {\n          this.ai1 = 539;                           // :60911-60913 史莱姆法杖\n        } else if (sf.getGoodWorld && yTile > surfaceY && roll(num4) === 0) {\n          this.ai1 = 147;                           // :60914-60916 ftw 地下 → 棘刺丛林史莱姆物\n        } else if (i === 0 && sf.remix && this.ai0 !== -999 && roll(3) === 0) {\n          this.ai1 = 75;                            // :60917-60919 首掷 remix → 生命水晶星史莱姆\n        }\n        // Main.vampireSeed（:60920-60923）：哈希秘密种子无载体，跳过\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** AI_001_Slimes 原版核（1.4.5.6 NPC.cs:60735+）：\n   *  站定 vx*=0.8；ai[0] 每 tick +1（激愤 +1 额外）；\n   *  阶段判定（num54=-1000）：ai0>=0 → 小跳（vy=-6, vx+=2*dir, ai0=-1120）；\n   *  -1000..-500 → 小跳（ai0=-2120）；-2000..-1500 → 大跳（vy=-8, vx+=3*dir, ai0=-200）。\n   *  即：小跳→小跳→大跳循环，约 5-10 秒节奏。\n   *  flag3 激愤（L61446）= 夜晚 || 受过伤 || 地下 → 追玩家；白天满血地表 = 被动保持方向。\n   *  卡墙（L61937）：落地 X == 起跳 X → direction 反转 + ai[2]=200 冷却（期间不索敌）。\n   *  蚱蜢(377/446) 复用本 AI（ai0 额外 +3，跳得更频繁） */\n  private slimeAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {"

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T09:58:48.142Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Now let me implement the three TownNPC items. I need to verify the exact vanilla semantics once more:

**Item 5 — Truffle variation (:93563-93567)**:
```
if (townNpcVariationIndex == 1) {
    float num7 = num4;   // num4 = 0.1
    num4 = num6;         // num6 = 0.6 + num3
    num6 = num7;         // R ↔ B swap
}
```
Then ×0.35 all. So shimmered Truffle: R = (0.6+n3)*0.35, B = 0.1*0.35.
Gate: `townNpcVariationIndex == 1` = IsShimmerVariant (requires ShimmerTownTransform[160] — and isNpcTownShimmerable(160) is true per the json, so `this.shimmered` is exactly the ==1 case for this NPC).

**Item 4 — Cyborg ai0==12 (:93594-93596)**:
```
if (ai[0] == 12f) Lighting.AddLight(base.Top, 0.3f, 0.1f, 0.1f);
```
This is an ADDITIONAL light at Top. Repo single slot → merge channels and place at Top. The base light (0.1, 0.3+n3/2, 0.4+n3/2)×0.35 at Center plus (0.3,0.1,0.1) at Top. With a single slot, the closest is channel-max with lightRGBAt = Top? But then the base light position changes... Hmm.

Look at how Enemy handles this: mergeLightRGB takes an optional `at` and only applies it when the slot was empty. For the Cyborg case, during the attack state the dominant new light is the red rocket glow at Top. 

Vanilla adds BOTH: center blue-ish + top red. The repo renderer reads lightRGB + lightRGBAt (single). Options:
(a) return channel-max [max(0.035,0.3), max(~0.14,0.1), max(~0.2,0.1)] with lightRGBAt=null (center) — loses the Top position.
(b) return the merged color at Top.

[1, 80 / 255, 40 / 255] : [40 / 255, 80 / 255, 1];\n      case 20: return this.shimmered ? [90 / 255, 20 / 255, 210 / 255] : [40 / 255, 1, 80 / 255];\n      case 663: return this.shimmered ? [1, 0, 77 / 255] : [1, 143 / 255, 197 / 255];\n      default: return [1, 1, 1];        // 集合缺省 Color.White（ToVector3 丢 alpha）\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 本 tick 点光（NPC.cs 城镇 NPC 侧三处恒亮）：\n   *  - 160 松露（UpdateNPC_CastLights :93544-93566）：num3=Next(28,42)×0.005+\n   *    (270−mouseTextColor)/500，基色 (0.1,0.3+num3/2,0.6+num3)×0.35；变体 1\n   *    （townNpcVariationIndex==1）R/B 互换——以 shimmered 承接（:93563-93567）\n   *  - 209 电子人（:93590-93599）：同式抖动 ×0.35、B 基 0.4；ai[0]==12（火箭射击\n   *    态，AttackType[209]=1 → ai0=12，NPC.cs:55902/:54936）追加 Top (0.3,0.1,0.1)\n   *    （:93594-93596）——单槽逐通道 max 合并（Enemy.mergeLightRGB 同款近似）\n   *  - 681 彩虹城镇史莱姆（AI_007 尾段 :55963-55974）= TorchColor(23)×0.35\n   *  - 683 黄城镇史莱姆（:55974-55979）= (0.25,0.25,0.1)×WrappedLerp(0.75,1,t%120/120)\n   *  条件态：魔法光环 ai[0]==14（:55394，num69=1 恒满档，atkState===14 承接）\n   *  ×GetMagicAuraColor；跳过两处——:53637 微光上升光（ai[1] 30→90 上升态计时\n   *  机未移植，本仓入微光即刻标记+传送回家，无上升过程）；:55546 ai[0]==24\n   *  （树妖 Stardew 演出 HaveDryadDoStardewAnimation :19123，公主礼物彩蛋无载体） */\n  get lightRGB(): [number, number, number] | null {\n    let rgb: [number, number, number] | null = null;\n    // 逐通道 max 合并（原版同帧多 AddLight，单槽近似视觉并集；不挪光源位）\n    const merge = (c: [number, number, number]) => {\n      rgb = rgb ? [Math.max(rgb[0], c[0]), Math.max(rgb[1], c[1]), Math.max(rgb[2], c[2])] : c;\n    };\n    if (this.vanillaId === 160) {\n      const n3 = (28 + Math.random() * 14) * 0.005 + (270 - mouseTextColorApprox()) / 500;\n      const base: [number, number, number] = [0.1 * 0.35, (0.3 + n3 / 2) * 0.35, (0.6 + n3) * 0.35];\n      // :93563-93567 变体 1（shimmered）→ R/B 互换（num7 交换 num4/num6）\n      merge(this.shimmered ? [base[2], base[1], base[0]] : base);\n    }\n    if (this.vanillaId === 209) {\n      const n3 = (28 + Math.random() * 14) * 0.005 + (270 - mouseTextColorApprox()) / 500;\n      merge([0.1 * 0.35, (0.3 + n3 / 2) * 0.35, (0.4 + n3 / 2) * 0.35]);\n      if (this.atkState === 12) merge([0.3, 0.1, 0.1]);   // :93594 火箭射击态 Top 追加光\n    }\n    if (this.vanillaId === 681) {\n      const num = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.9 + (270 - mouseTextColorApprox()) / 900));\n      merge([num * 0.9 * 0.35, 0.55 * 0.35, num * 1.2 * 0.35]);   // TorchColor(23)×0.35\n    }\n    if (this.vanillaId === 683) {\n      const p = (visEffectFrames() % 120) / 120;\n      const tri = p * 2 > 1 ? 2 - p * 2 : p * 2;                   // Utils.WrappedLerp\n      const l = 0.75 + 0.25 * tri;\n      merge([0.25 * l, 0.25 * l, 0.1 * l]);\n    }\n    // 魔法光环（:55390-55396）：ai[0]==14 魔法攻击态内 num69=1 恒满档 → GetMagicAuraColor\n    if (this.atkState === 14\n      && (this.vanillaId === 54 || this.vanillaId === 108 || this.vanillaId === 160\n        || this.vanillaId === 20 || this.vanillaId === 663)) {\n      merge(this.magicAuraColor());\n    }\n    return rgb;\n  }"
}

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';

// 小动物 AI 全家族 1:1 回归（aiStyle 1/7/16/64/65/66/67/68/112/114/115/116/118）
function critterWorld(playerAt: { x: number; y: number } | null, opts: { water?: boolean } = {}) {
  const w = new World(80, 80, 42, 'open');
  w.groundLevel = 42; // 真实世界由生成期赋值；测试默认 0 会让"地下"判定恒真
  const st = w.store;
  for (let x = 10; x <= 70; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, 60, 2); // 地板 y=60
  if (opts.water) {
    // y 45-59 注水（水面在 45）
    for (let x = 20; x <= 60; x++) for (let y = 45; y <= 59; y++) st.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 1);
  }
  const hooks = {
    world: w,
    player: playerAt ? {
      x: playerAt.x * 16, y: playerAt.y * 16, w: 20, h: 42,
      cx: playerAt.x * 16, cy: playerAt.y * 16 + 21,
      dead: false, useTime: 0,
    } : null,
    playSfx: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, spawnParticles: () => {},
    spawnDrop: () => null, damagePlayer: () => {},
    entities: { add: () => {} },
    enemies: () => [] as unknown[],
  } as never;
  return { w, hooks };
}

    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(46, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    let walked = false, jumped = false;
    for (let t = 0; t < 3000; t++) {
      e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
      if (Math.abs(e.vx) > 0.2) walked = true;
      if (e.vy < -1) jumped = true;

    expect(jumped).toBe(true);
  });

// 掉落系统回归（对照 1.4.5.6 ItemDropDatabase 提取表 + NPC 掉落管线）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';
import {
  resolveDrops, rollCoins, rollHeartsAndStars, npcValueOf, evalCond,
  type NpcDropCtx,
} from '../src/drops/NpcDrops';
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID } from '../src/data/items';
import dropData from '../src/data/vanilla-npcdrops.json';

function mkCtx(over: Partial<NpcDropCtx> = {}): NpcDropCtx {
  return {
    rng: new RNG(42),
    netId: 1, type: 1,
    isExpert: false, isMaster: false, hardMode: false, crimson: false,
    flags: {},
    x: 1000, y: 1000,
    worldSurfaceTile: 200, rockLayerTile: 400, underworldTileY: 900,
    maxTilesX: 4200, maxTilesY: 1200,
    lifeMax: 50, friendly: false, boss: false, damage: 10, value: 10,
    hp: 80, hpMax: 100, mana: 15, manaMax: 20,
    ...over,
  };
}

/** 采样 N 次击杀：每杀独立 rng，返回 id→总件数 与 每杀明细（互斥断言用） */
function sample(type: number, n: number, over: Partial<NpcDropCtx> = {}) {
  const counts = new Map<number, number>();
  const perKill: Array<number[]> = [];
  let zeroValue = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const c = mkCtx({ rng: new RNG(i * 7919 + 13), netId: type, type, ...over });
    const res = resolveDrops(c);
    zeroValue = zeroValue || res.zeroValue;
    const ids: number[] = [];
    for (const d of res.drops) {
      counts.set(d.id, (counts.get(d.id) ?? 0) + d.n);
      ids.push(d.id);
    }
    perKill.push(ids);
  }
  return { counts, perKill, zeroValue };
}

    const npcs = new Set<number>();
    const kinds = new Set<string>();

      cs.reduce((s, c) => s + c.n * (c.id === 71 ? 1 : c.id === 72 ? 100 : c.id === 73 ? 10000 : 1000000), 0);
    const mean = (luck: number) => {
      let s = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3000; i++) s += total(rollCoins(1000, mkCtx({ rng: new RNG(i * 13 + 7), luck }), false));
      return s / 3000;
    };
    const base = mean(0), good = mean(1), bad = mean(-1);
    expect(good).toBeGreaterThan(base * 1.03);   // luck>0 取两轮较大
    expect(bad).toBeLessThan(base * 0.97);       // luck<0 取两轮较小
